    WHEN [t].[MaTheLoai] IS NOT NULL THEN [t].[TenTheLoai]
    ELSE N'Chưa phân loại'
END AS [TenTheLoai], [s0].[AnhBia], [s0].[SoLuongKhaDung]
FROM (
    SELECT TOP(@__p_0) [s].[MaSach], [s].[AnhBia], [s].[MaTheLoai], [s].[NgayTao], [s].[SoLuongKhaDung], [s].[TenSach]

    ORDER BY [s].[NgayTao] DESC

END AS [TenTheLoai], [s0].[AnhBia], [s0].[SoLuongKhaDung]
FROM (
    SELECT TOP(@__p_0) [s].[MaSach], [s].[AnhBia], [s].[MaTheLoai], [s].[NgayTao], [s].[SoLuongKhaDung], [s].[TenSach]

) AS [s0]

ORDER BY [s0].[NgayTao] DESC
